Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the combinatorics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 780. The resulting operator achieves bound O(342/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the combinatorics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^780 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 342-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
